Util.FX = (function()
	local FX = {}
	local RealTweenService = TweenService
	local Lighting = cloneref(game:GetService("Lighting"))

	FX.Settings = {Blur = true, Transitions = true, ExtraSounds = true, Ambient = true}

	local Quint, Quad, Back, Linear = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear
	local Bounce = Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce
	local EOut, EIn, EInOut = Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingDirection.In, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
	local WHITE = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� CUSTOMIZATION (saved in SaveData.Custom) â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	-- 10 triangle + 10 intro + 10 notification + 5 extra UI options.
	-- Everything below reads FX.Cfg live, so changes apply instantly.
	local DEFAULT_CFG = {
		-- triangle
		TriColorMode = 1, TriColor = "#FFFFFF", TriSize = 1, TriSpin = 1, TriFaceTr = 0.38, TriEdge = 1,
		TriNodes = true, TriFaces = true, TriMusic = true, TriExplode = true,
		-- intro
		IntroTitle = "STEVETHEREALONE", IntroSub = "", IntroMarquee = "", IntroColorMode = 1, IntroColor = "#FFFFFF",
		IntroStars = true, IntroRays = true, IntroScan = true, IntroHud = true, IntroIntensity = 1,
		-- notifications
		NotifMode = 1, NotifDuration = 4, NotifTitle = "Nooby's Uh Modded Reanimation", NotifPos = 1, NotifWidth = 300,
		NotifSize = 18, NotifProgress = true, NotifColor = "", NotifSound = false, NotifSpeed = 1,
		-- extra ui
		UIBgTr = 0, UIBorder = 1, UIRound = 5, UIGlow = true, UIAccent = "",
	}
	FX.Cfg = {}
	for k, v in pairs(DEFAULT_CFG) do
		FX.Cfg[k] = v
	end
	FX.CfgDefaults = DEFAULT_CFG
	function FX.InitCustom(SaveData)
		if type(SaveData.Custom) ~= "table" then
			SaveData.Custom = {}
		end
		FX.Store = SaveData.Custom
		for k, d in pairs(DEFAULT_CFG) do
			local v = FX.Store[k]
			if type(v) ~= type(d) then
				v = d
			end
			FX.Cfg[k] = v
		end
	end
	function FX.SetCfg(key, value)
		FX.Cfg[key] = value
		if FX.Store then
			FX.Store[key] = value
		end
	end
	function FX.ResetCfg()
		for k, d in pairs(DEFAULT_CFG) do
			FX.SetCfg(k, d)
		end
	end
	function FX.TriColor(t)
		local C = FX.Cfg
		if C.TriColorMode == 2 then
			return Util.ParseHex(C.TriColor) or WHITE
		elseif C.TriColorMode == 3 then
			return Color3.fromHSV((t * 0.2) % 1, 0.8, 1)
		end
		return FX.GetColor and FX.GetColor() or Color3.fromHSV((t / 10) % 1, 0.8, 1)
	end
	function FX.MarqueeText(fallback)
		local m = FX.Cfg.IntroMarquee
		if type(m) == "string" and m ~= "" then
			return (m:gsub("[^\32-\126]", "")) .. string.rep(" ", 24)
		end
		return fallback
	end

	-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
	local function tween(obj, time, style, dir, props)
		local t = RealTweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(time, style or Quint, dir or EOut), props)
		t:Play()
		return t
	end
	local function easeOutCubic(x)
		return 1 - (1 - x) ^ 3
	end
	local function easeInOut(x)
		if x < 0.5 then
			return 4 * x * x * x
		end
		return 1 - ((-2 * x + 2) ^ 3) / 2
	end
	local function easeOutBack(x)
		local c1 = 1.70158
		local c3 = c1 + 1
		return 1 + c3 * (x - 1) ^ 3 + c1 * (x - 1) ^ 2
	end
	local function isPress(input)
		return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
	end

	-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ screen blur â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
	local Blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
	Blur.Name = Util.RandomString(16)
	Blur.Size = 0
	do
		local ok = pcall(function()
			Blur.Parent = Lighting
		end)
		if not ok then
			pcall(function()
				Blur.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera
			end)
		end
	end
	local blurToken = 0
	function FX.BlurPulse(peak, inTime, outTime)
		if not FX.Settings.Blur then return end
		blurToken = blurToken + 1
		local my = blurToken
		inTime = inTime or 0.15
		tween(Blur, inTime, Quad, EOut, {Size = peak})
		task.delay(inTime, function()
			if my ~= blurToken then return end
			tween(Blur, outTime or 0.45, Quad, EIn, {Size = 0})
		end)
	end

	-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ sounds â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
	FX.UISound = nil
	-- {pitch, volume}. Built from the script's own click sound so it follows themes + mute.
	local presets = {
		click = {1, 1}, tick = {1.9, 0.2}, release = {1.5, 0.28}, open = {0.75, 0.85}, back = {0.62, 0.85},
		close = {1.3, 0.8}, boom = {0.42, 1.4}, reform = {1.6, 0.9}, hit = {1.2, 0.9},
	}
	function FX.Play(kind, pitchMul, volMul)
		if not FX.Settings.ExtraSounds then return end
		local U = FX.UISound
		local base = U and U.Click
		if not base or base.Volume <= 0 then return end
		local p = presets[kind] or presets.click
		local s = base:Clone()
		s.PlaybackSpeed = p[1] * (pitchMul or 1) * (0.96 + math.random() * 0.08)
		s.Volume = base.Volume * p[2] * (volMul or 1)
		s.Parent = base.Parent
		s:Play()
		Debris:AddItem(s, 2.5)
	end

	-- â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ tiny fx: rings + sparks â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
	function FX.Ring(parent, color, size, time, thick, pos)
		local r = Util.Instance("Frame", parent)
		r.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		r.Position = pos or UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
		r.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
		r.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		r.BorderSizePixel = 0
		r.ZIndex = 30
		r.Interactable = false
		Util.Instance("UICorner", r).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		local s = Util.Instance("UIStroke", r)
		s.Color = color
		s.Thickness = thick or 3
		s.Transparency = 0.1
		tween(r, time, Quint, EOut, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)})
		tween(s, time, Quad, EOut, {Transparency = 1, Thickness = 0.5})
		Debris:AddItem(r, time + 0.1)
		return r
	end
	function FX.Sparks(parent, color, n, dist, time)
		for _ = 1, n do
			local f = Util.Instance("Frame", parent)
			f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			f.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3)
			f.BackgroundColor3 = color
			f.BorderSizePixel = 0
			f.ZIndex = 30
			f.Interactable = false
			local a = math.random() * math.pi * 2
			local d = dist * (0.45 + math.random() * 0.55)
			tween(f, time, Quint, EOut, {
				Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(a) * d, 0.5, math.sin(a) * d),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
			})
			Debris:AddItem(f, time + 0.1)
		end
	end

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� 3D TRIANGLE ENGINE â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	local TET_E = 2
	local TET_R = TET_E / math.sqrt(3)
	local TET_H = TET_E * math.sqrt(2 / 3)
	local TET = {
		Vector3.new(0, TET_H * 0.75, 0),
		Vector3.new(TET_R, -TET_H * 0.25, 0),
		Vector3.new(TET_R * math.cos(math.rad(120)), -TET_H * 0.25, TET_R * math.sin(math.rad(120))),
		Vector3.new(TET_R * math.cos(math.rad(240)), -TET_H * 0.25, TET_R * math.sin(math.rad(240))),
	}
	local TET_FACES = {{2, 3, 4}, {1, 2, 3}, {1, 3, 4}, {1, 4, 2}}
	local TET_EDGES = {{1, 2}, {1, 3}, {1, 4}, {2, 3}, {3, 4}, {4, 2}}
	local ACTIONS = {"SPIN", "PULSE", "FLIP", "PRISM", "BLOOM"}
	local FAR = CFrame.new(0, 1e4, 0)

	local function newPart(class, parent)
		local p = Instance.new(class)
		p.Name = Util.RandomString(12)
		p.Anchored = true
		p.CanCollide = false
		p.CanQuery = false
		p.CanTouch = false
		p.CastShadow = false
		p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
		p.Parent = parent
		return p
	end
	local function rndUnit()
		local v = Vector3.new(math.random() * 2 - 1, math.random() * 2 - 1, math.random() * 2 - 1)
		if v.Magnitude < 0.05 then v = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
		return v.Unit
	end
	local function rndOut(base)
		local d = base.Magnitude > 0.01 and base.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
		return (d * 1.4 + rndUnit()).Unit
	end

	-- draws a triangle (a,b,c) with two thin wedges
	local function drawTri(a, b, c, w1, w2, th)
		local ab, ac, bc = b - a, c - a, c - b
		if ac:Cross(ab).Magnitude < 1e-5 then
			w1.CFrame = FAR
			w2.CFrame = FAR
			return
		end
		local abd, acd, bcd = ab:Dot(ab), ac:Dot(ac), bc:Dot(bc)
		if abd > acd and abd > bcd then
			c, a = a, c
		elseif acd > bcd and acd > abd then
			a, b = b, a
		end
		ab, ac, bc = b - a, c - a, c - b
		local right = ac:Cross(ab).Unit
		local up = bc:Cross(right).Unit
		local back = bc.Unit
		local height = math.abs(ab:Dot(up))
		w1.Size = Vector3.new(th, math.max(height, 0.001), math.max(math.abs(ab:Dot(back)), 0.001))
		w1.CFrame = CFrame.fromMatrix((a + b) / 2, right, up, back)
		w2.Size = Vector3.new(th, math.max(height, 0.001), math.max(math.abs(ac:Dot(back)), 0.001))
		w2.CFrame = CFrame.fromMatrix((a + c) / 2, -right, up, -back)
	end

	function FX.NewTriangle3D(parent, opts)
		opts = opts or {}
		local CFG = FX.Cfg
		local T = {
			scaleBase = 1, chargeVis = 0, baseSpin = 0.9,
			yaw = 0.6, pitch = 0.35, yawVel = 3,
			flipBase = 0, flipAngle = 0, flipT = nil,
			pulse = 0, pulseAmt = 0, prism = 0, bloomT = nil, bloomEnv = 0,
			charge = 0, holdT = 0, hot = 0, holding = false, dragging = false,
			phase = "idle", phaseT = 0, env = 0, extra = 0,
			clickN = 0, active = true, pop = nil, popScale = 1,
			pressAmt = 0, hoverAmt = 0, hover = false, trackX = 0, trackY = 0, lastLoud = 0,
			color = WHITE,
		}
		local conns = {}

		local layer = Util.Instance("Frame", parent)
		layer.AnchorPoint = opts.LayerAnchor or Vector2.zero
		layer.Position = opts.LayerPos or UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
		layer.Size = opts.LayerSize or UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		layer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		layer.BorderSizePixel = 0
		layer.ZIndex = opts.ZIndex or 3
		-- NOTE: never set Interactable=false here. Interactable is inherited by descendants, which is
		-- exactly what used to kill the hit-button (taps / the 5s hold never registered).
		layer.Active = false
		T.Layer = layer

		local vp = Util.Instance("ViewportFrame", layer)
		vp.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		vp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		vp.BorderSizePixel = 0
		vp.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 145)
		vp.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		vp.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.4, -0.9, -0.6)
		local cam = Util.Instance("Camera", vp)
		cam.FieldOfView = 35
		cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 5.4)
		vp.CurrentCamera = cam
		local model = Util.Instance("Model", vp)

		-- pieces
		local faces, edges, nodes = {}, {}, {}
		for i, idx in ipairs(TET_FACES) do
			local a, b, c = TET[idx[1]], TET[idx[2]], TET[idx[3]]
			faces[i] = {v = {a, b, c}, center = (a + b + c) / 3, w1 = newPart("WedgePart", model), w2 = newPart("WedgePart", model)}
		end
		for i, pair in ipairs(TET_EDGES) do
			local a, b = TET[pair[1]], TET[pair[2]]
			local part = newPart("Part", model)
			part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			edges[i] = {a = a, b = b, mid = (a + b) / 2, part = part}
		end
		for i = 1, 4 do
			local part = newPart("Part", model)
			part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
			part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			nodes[i] = {pos = TET[i], part = part}
		end
		local function reseed()
			for _, f in ipairs(faces) do
				f.dir, f.axis, f.spin = rndOut(f.center), rndUnit(), (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 7
			end
			for _, e in ipairs(edges) do
				e.dir, e.axis, e.spin = rndOut(e.mid), rndUnit(), (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 11
			end
			for _, n in ipairs(nodes) do
				n.dir = rndOut(n.pos)
			end
		end
		reseed()

		-- â”€â”€ optional segmented charge ring (the hold gauge) â”€â”€
		local RING_N = 40
		local ringHost, ringSegs = nil, {}
		if opts.Ring then
			ringHost = Util.Instance("Frame", layer)
			ringHost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			ringHost.Position = opts.HitPos or UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			local rs = opts.RingSize or 190
			ringHost.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(rs, rs)
			ringHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			ringHost.BorderSizePixel = 0
			ringHost.ZIndex = 4
			for i = 1, RING_N do
				local a = (i - 1) / RING_N * math.pi * 2 - math.pi / 2
				local s = Util.Instance("Frame", ringHost)
				s.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				s.Position = UDim2.new(0.5 + math.cos(a) * 0.5, 0, 0.5 + math.sin(a) * 0.5, 0)
				s.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 2)
				s.Rotation = math.deg(a)
				s.BorderSizePixel = 0
				s.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
				s.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
				s.ZIndex = 4
				ringSegs[i] = s
			end
		end

		-- â”€â”€ render the current state â”€â”€
		local D = 2.3
		local function draw(t)
			local e = T.env + T.bloomEnv * 0.28
			local S = math.max(0.001, T.scaleBase * T.popScale * (1 + T.pulseAmt) * (1 - 0.08 * T.pressAmt) * CFG.TriSize)
			local M = CFrame.Angles(T.pitch + T.flipAngle, T.yaw, 0)
			local sh = T.hot * T.hot * 0.07
			if sh > 0 then
				M = M + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * sh * 2, (math.random() - 0.5) * sh * 2, 0)
			end
			local spinMul = 1 + 0.5 * T.extra
			local th = (0.045 + T.hot * 0.05) * CFG.TriEdge
			local base = T.color
			local hue = (t * 0.8) % 1
			local fadeOut = e > 0 and math.min(1, e) * 0.12 or 0

			for i, f in ipairs(faces) do
				local rot = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(f.axis, f.spin * e * spinMul)
				local c = f.center
				local off = f.dir * (D * e)
				local p1 = M * ((c + off + rot * (f.v[1] - c)) * S)
				local p2 = M * ((c + off + rot * (f.v[2] - c)) * S)
				local p3 = M * ((c + off + rot * (f.v[3] - c)) * S)
				drawTri(p1, p2, p3, f.w1, f.w2, 0.03)
				local col = base
				if T.prism > 0 then
					col = Color3.fromHSV((hue + i * 0.22) % 1, 0.75, 1):Lerp(base, 1 - T.prism)
				end
				col = col:Lerp(WHITE, (i - 1) * 0.07 + T.hot * 0.5)
				local tr = CFG.TriFaces and math.clamp(CFG.TriFaceTr - T.hot * 0.22 + fadeOut, 0, 1) or 1
				f.w1.Color, f.w2.Color = col, col
				f.w1.Transparency, f.w2.Transparency = tr, tr
			end

			local edgeCol = WHITE:Lerp(base, 0.3 * (1 - T.hot))
			for _, ed in ipairs(edges) do
				local rot = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(ed.axis, ed.spin * e * spinMul)
				local off = ed.dir * (D * e * 1.15)
				local a = M * ((ed.mid + off + rot * (ed.a - ed.mid)) * S)
				local b = M * ((ed.mid + off + rot * (ed.b - ed.mid)) * S)
				local len = (b - a).Magnitude
				local part = ed.part
				if len < 1e-3 then
					part.CFrame = FAR
				else
					part.Size = Vector3.new(th, th, len)
					part.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((a + b) / 2, b)
				end
				part.Color = edgeCol
			end

			local nodeSize = 0.14 + T.hot * 0.12 + T.pulse * 0.06
			for _, nd in ipairs(nodes) do
				if CFG.TriNodes then
					local p = M * ((nd.pos + nd.dir * (D * e * 1.3)) * S)
					nd.part.Size = Vector3.new(nodeSize, nodeSize, nodeSize)
					nd.part.CFrame = CFrame.new(p)
					nd.part.Color = base:Lerp(WHITE, 0.55)
				else
					nd.part.CFrame = FAR
				end
			end
		end

		-- â”€â”€ actions â”€â”€
		local function caption(name, n)
			if opts.OnAction then opts.OnAction(name, n) end
		end
		function T:Bloom()
			self.bloomT = 0
		end
		function T:PopIn()
			self.pop = 0
			self.popScale = 0.001
		end
		function T:Explode()
			if self.phase ~= "idle" then return end
			reseed()
			self.phase, self.phaseT = "explode", 0
			self.holding, self.holdT, self.charge = false, 0, 1
			FX.BlurPulse(11, 0.08, 0.9)
			FX.Play("boom")
			FX.Ring(layer, self.color, 320, 0.8, 4)
			FX.Ring(layer, WHITE, 200, 0.55, 2)
			FX.Sparks(layer, self.color, 22, 150, 0.9)
			caption("OVERLOAD", 0)
		end
		function T:Reformed()
			FX.Play("reform")
			FX.BlurPulse(5, 0.06, 0.5)
			FX.Ring(layer, self.color, 240, 0.7, 3)
			FX.Sparks(layer, WHITE, 14, 110, 0.7)
			self.yawVel = self.yawVel + 9
			self.pulse = 1
			caption("REFORMED", 0)
		end
		function T:Finale()
			self.yawVel = self.yawVel + 18
			self.pulse = 1
			FX.Ring(layer, self.color, 260, 0.7, 3)
			FX.Play("reform", 1.1)
		end
		function T:Click()
			if self.phase ~= "idle" then return end
			self.clickN = (self.clickN % 5) + 1
			local n = self.clickN
			local c = self.color
			if n == 1 then -- SPIN
				self.yawVel = self.yawVel + 15
				FX.Play("click", 1.25)
			elseif n == 2 then -- PULSE
				self.pulse = 1
				FX.Ring(layer, c, 180, 0.6, 3)
				FX.Play("click", 0.8)
			elseif n == 3 then -- FLIP
				self.flipT = 0
				FX.Play("hit", 0.9)
			elseif n == 4 then -- PRISM
				self.prism = 1
				FX.Sparks(layer, c, 16, 120, 0.8)
				FX.Play("hit", 1.5)
			else -- BLOOM
				self.bloomT = 0
				FX.Ring(layer, WHITE, 220, 0.7, 2)
				FX.Play("open", 1.2)
			end
			caption(ACTIONS[n], n)
		end

		-- â”€â”€ per-frame simulation â”€â”€
		function T:Step(dt)
			if not self.active then return end
			local t = os.clock()
			self.color = FX.TriColor(t)

			local explodeHot = 0
			if self.phase == "explode" then
				self.phaseT = self.phaseT + dt
				self.env = easeOutCubic(math.min(1, self.phaseT / 0.85))
				self.extra = math.min(1, self.phaseT / 1.2)
				explodeHot = math.max(0, 1 - self.phaseT / 1.0) * 0.8
				if self.phaseT >= 1.15 then
					self.phase, self.phaseT = "reform", 0
				end
			elseif self.phase == "reform" then
				self.phaseT = self.phaseT + dt
				local u = math.min(1, self.phaseT / 1.1)
				self.env = 1 - easeOutBack(u)
				self.extra = 1 - u
				if u >= 1 then
					self.phase, self.env, self.extra = "idle", 0, 0
					self:Reformed()
				end
			end

			if self.holding and self.phase == "idle" and CFG.TriExplode then
				self.holdT = self.holdT + dt
				self.charge = math.min(1, self.holdT / 5)
				if self.holdT >= 5 then
					self:Explode()
				end
			else
				self.charge = math.max(0, self.charge - dt * 1.4)
			end

			if self.flipT then
				self.flipT = self.flipT + dt / 0.75
				if self.flipT >= 1 then
					self.flipBase = (self.flipBase + math.pi) % (math.pi * 2)
					self.flipT = nil
				end
			end
			self.flipAngle = self.flipBase + (self.flipT and easeOutBack(self.flipT) * math.pi or 0)

			self.pulse = math.max(0, self.pulse - dt * 1.8)
			self.pulseAmt = 0.3 * self.pulse * math.cos((1 - self.pulse) * math.pi * 3)
			self.prism = math.max(0, self.prism - dt / 2.2)
			if self.bloomT then
				self.bloomT = self.bloomT + dt / 0.9
				if self.bloomT >= 1 then
					self.bloomT, self.bloomEnv = nil, 0
				else
					self.bloomEnv = math.sin(self.bloomT * math.pi)
				end
			end
			if self.pop then
				self.pop = self.pop + dt / 0.8
				if self.pop >= 1 then
					self.pop, self.popScale = nil, 1
				else
					self.popScale = easeOutBack(self.pop)
				end
			end

			local pTarget = (self.holding and self.phase == "idle") and 1 or 0
			self.pressAmt = self.pressAmt + (pTarget - self.pressAmt) * (1 - math.exp(-14 * dt))
			self.hoverAmt = self.hoverAmt + ((self.hover and 1 or 0) - self.hoverAmt) * (1 - math.exp(-9 * dt))
			if opts.Music and CFG.TriMusic and self.phase == "idle" then
				local pbl = opts.Music.PlaybackLoudness
				if pbl - self.lastLoud > 24 then
					self.pulse = math.max(self.pulse, 0.5)
				end
				self.lastLoud = pbl
			end
			self.hot = math.max(self.charge, self.chargeVis, self.pulse * 0.5, explodeHot, self.hoverAmt * 0.22)
			if opts.Track and self.phase == "idle" then
				local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				local ap, as = layer.AbsolutePosition, layer.AbsoluteSize
				local tx = math.clamp((m.X - (ap.X + as.X * 0.5)) / 240, -1, 1)
				local ty = math.clamp((m.Y - (ap.Y + as.Y * 0.5)) / 160, -1, 1)
				self.trackX = self.trackX + (tx - self.trackX) * (1 - math.exp(-5 * dt))
				self.trackY = self.trackY + (ty - self.trackY) * (1 - math.exp(-5 * dt))
			end
			if not self.dragging then
				local spinTarget = self.baseSpin * CFG.TriSpin + self.trackX * 1.1
				self.yawVel = spinTarget + (self.yawVel - spinTarget) * math.exp(-1.5 * dt)
				self.yaw = self.yaw + self.yawVel * dt * (1 + self.hot * 2)
				local basePitch = 0.35 + 0.1 * math.sin(t * 1.2) + self.trackY * 0.45
				self.pitch = self.pitch + (basePitch - self.pitch) * (1 - math.exp(-1.5 * dt))
			end
			if ringHost then
				ringHost.Rotation = (t * (6 + self.hot * 40)) % 360
				local lit = math.floor(self.charge * RING_N + 0.5)
				if self.phase ~= "idle" then lit = RING_N end
				local litCol = self.color:Lerp(WHITE, 0.3 + self.hot * 0.4)
				for i = 1, RING_N do
					local seg = ringSegs[i]
					if i <= lit then
						seg.BackgroundColor3 = litCol
						seg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
						seg.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 2)
					else
						seg.BackgroundColor3 = self.color
						seg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88 - self.hoverAmt * 0.3
						seg.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 2)
					end
				end
			end
			draw(t)
		end

		-- â”€â”€ input (drag / click / hold) â”€â”€
		-- FIXED: the hit-button used to sit under an Interactable=false layer (dead input), and the hold
		-- was cancelled by accumulated mouse PATH LENGTH (normal hand jitter killed the 5s charge).
		-- Now the drag deadzone is NET displacement and each press is tracked by its input object.
		if opts.Interactive then
			local hit = Util.Instance("TextButton", layer)
			hit.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			hit.Position = opts.HitPos or UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			hit.Size = opts.HitSize or UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			hit.BorderSizePixel = 0
			hit.Text = ""
			hit.AutoButtonColor = false
			hit.Active = true
			hit.Selectable = false
			hit.ZIndex = 20
			T.Hit = hit
			local press = {down = false, t0 = 0, disp = Vector2.zero, moved = false, input = nil}
			local DEADZONE = 28

			local function samePress(input)
				if not press.input then return false end
				if input == press.input then return true end
				return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
					and press.input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			end
			local function endPress(cancel)
				if not press.down then return end
				press.down = false
				local dur = os.clock() - press.t0
				local wasHold = T.holdT
				T.dragging = false
				T.holding, T.holdT = false, 0
				FX.TriangleDown = false
				FX.TriangleLast = os.clock()
				if cancel or T.phase ~= "idle" then return end
				if not press.moved and dur < 0.6 then
					T:Click()
				elseif not press.moved and wasHold > 0.6 then
					-- released a charge early: fizzle so the player knows it needs the full 5s
					FX.Play("release", 0.8)
					FX.Ring(layer, T.color, 120, 0.4, 2, opts.HitPos)
					caption("HOLD LONGER", 0)
				end
			end

			hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if not isPress(input) or press.down then return end
				if T.phase ~= "idle" then return end
				press.down, press.t0, press.disp, press.moved, press.input = true, os.clock(), Vector2.zero, false, input
				T.holding, T.holdT = true, 0
				FX.TriangleDown = true
				FX.TriangleLast = os.clock()
				FX.Play("tick", 0.9)
			end)
			hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				T.hover = true
			end)
			hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				T.hover = false
			end)
			conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
				if not press.down then return end
				local ty = input.UserInputType
				if ty ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and ty ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
				if ty == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input ~= press.input then return end
				local d = input.Delta
				press.disp = press.disp + Vector2.new(d.X, d.Y)
				if not press.moved and press.disp.Magnitude > DEADZONE then
					press.moved = true
					T.holding, T.holdT = false, 0 -- a real drag cancels the hold
				end
				if press.moved and T.phase == "idle" then
					T.dragging = true
					T.yaw = T.yaw + d.X * 0.012
					T.pitch = math.clamp(T.pitch + d.Y * 0.012, -1.3, 1.3)
					T.yawVel = math.clamp(d.X * 0.6, -16, 16)
				end
			end)
			conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
				if press.down and samePress(input) then
					endPress(false)
				end
			end)
			-- safety net: losing window focus mid-press must never leave the hold stuck
			conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function()
				endPress(true)
			end)
		end

		function T:SetActive(a)
			self.active = a
			vp.Visible = a
			if ringHost then ringHost.Visible = a end
			if not a then
				self.holding, self.holdT, self.charge = false, 0, 0
				self.hover = false
			end
		end
		function T:Destroy()
			for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
				c:Disconnect()
			end
			FX.TriangleDown = false
			layer:Destroy()
		end

		conns[#conns + 1] = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			if opts.IsVisible and not opts.IsVisible() then
				if T.holding then T.holding, T.holdT = false, 0 end
				return
			end
			local ok, err = pcall(T.Step, T, dt)
			if not ok then
				T.active = false
				warn("[UhhhhhhFX] triangle disabled: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end)
		return T
	end

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� BUTTON FX â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	local rippleHosts = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local function ripple(b, input)
		local host = rippleHosts[b]
		if not host or not host.Parent then
			host = Util.Instance("Frame", b)
			host.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			host.BorderSizePixel = 0
			host.ZIndex = 0
			host.ClipsDescendants = true
			host.Interactable = false
			Util.Instance("UICorner", host).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
			rippleHosts[b] = host
		end
		local mp
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			mp = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
		else
			mp = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		end
		local rel = mp - host.AbsolutePosition
		local c = Util.Instance("Frame", host)
		c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		c.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(rel.X, 0, host.AbsoluteSize.X), math.clamp(rel.Y, 0, host.AbsoluteSize.Y))
		c.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		c.BackgroundColor3 = FX.GetColor and FX.GetColor() or WHITE
		c.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
		c.BorderSizePixel = 0
		c.Interactable = false
		Util.Instance("UICorner", c).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		local big = math.max(host.AbsoluteSize.X, 60) * 2.2
		tween(c, 0.55, Quint, EOut, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(big, big), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		Debris:AddItem(c, 0.6)
	end
	local sheenFns = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	function FX.Sheen(b)
		local f = sheenFns[b]
		if f then f() end
	end
	-- staggered light-sweep across the first buttons of a page as it enters (NEW)
	function FX.Cascade(page)
		if not FX.Settings.Transitions then return end
		local n = 0
		for _, d in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("TextButton") and d:GetAttribute("FXB") then
				n = n + 1
				if n > 14 then break end
				task.delay(0.1 + n * 0.05, function()
					FX.Sheen(d)
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	function FX.EnhanceButton(b)
		if b:GetAttribute("FXB") then return end
		b:SetAttribute("FXB", true)
		local sc = Util.Instance("UIScale", b)
		local stroke = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
		local hover, down = false, false

		-- depth: soft vertical gradient (multiplies the themed colour; the label is a child so it is unaffected)
		local depth = Util.Instance("UIGradient", b)
		depth.Rotation = 90
		depth.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(212, 212, 212))

		-- lazily built: diagonal light streak + accent edge bar
		local rec = nil
		local function getRec()
			if rec and rec.host.Parent then return rec end
			local host = Util.Instance("Frame", b)
			host.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			host.BorderSizePixel = 0
			host.ZIndex = 0
			host.ClipsDescendants = true
			host.Interactable = false
			Util.Instance("UICorner", host).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
			local streak = Util.Instance("Frame", host)
			streak.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			streak.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
			streak.BorderSizePixel = 0
			streak.Interactable = false
			local g = Util.Instance("UIGradient", streak)
			g.Rotation = 25
			g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.72),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
			})
			g.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
			local bar = Util.Instance("Frame", host)
			bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			bar.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
			bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)
			bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
			bar.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
			bar.Interactable = false
			rec = {host = host, grad = g, bar = bar}
			return rec
		end
		local function sheen()
			if not FX.Settings.Transitions then return end
			local r = getRec()
			r.grad.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
			tween(r.grad, 0.6, Quad, EOut, {Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0)})
		end
		sheenFns[b] = function()
			pcall(sheen)
		end

		local function refresh(style, time)
			local s = 1
			if down then
				s = 0.955
			elseif hover then
				s = 1.03
			end
			tween(sc, time, style, EOut, {Scale = s})
		end
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if not FX.Settings.Transitions then return end
			hover = true
			refresh(Quad, 0.15)
			if stroke then tween(stroke, 0.15, Quad, EOut, {Thickness = 2}) end
			local r = getRec()
			r.bar.BackgroundColor3 = FX.GetColor and FX.GetColor() or WHITE
			tween(r.bar, 0.22, Quint, EOut, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -8)})
			sheen()
			FX.Play("tick")
		end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			hover, down = false, false
			refresh(Quad, 0.2)
			if stroke then tween(stroke, 0.2, Quad, EOut, {Thickness = 1}) end
			if rec then tween(rec.bar, 0.18, Quad, EOut, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)}) end
		end)
		b.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if not isPress(input) or not FX.Settings.Transitions then return end
			down = true
			refresh(Quad, 0.07)
			ripple(b, input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then sheen() end
		end)
		b.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if not isPress(input) or not down then return end
			down = false
			refresh(Back, 0.3)
			FX.Play("release")
		end)
	end

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� PAGE TRANSITIONS â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	local CENTER = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	local PageStyle = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local Rigs = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local Active = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local styleOrder = {1, 7, 3, 9, 4, 8, 5, 10, 6, 2}
	local styleCount = 0
	local veil, veilToken = nil, 0

	local function assignStyle(page, forced)
		if forced then
			PageStyle[page] = forced
			return forced
		end
		styleCount = styleCount + 1
		local s = styleOrder[(styleCount - 1) % #styleOrder + 1]
		PageStyle[page] = s
		return s
	end
	local function getRig(page)
		local r = Rigs[page]
		if r and r.scale.Parent then return r end
		r = {scale = Util.Instance("UIScale", page)}
		r.scale.Scale = 1
		Rigs[page] = r
		return r
	end
	local function resetVeilGeometry()
		if not veil then return end
		veil.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		veil.Position = CENTER
		veil.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	end
	local function getVeil()
		if not veil or not veil.Parent then
			veil = Util.Instance("Frame", FX.Content)
			veil.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			veil.BorderSizePixel = 0
			veil.ZIndex = 100
			veil.Interactable = false
			veil.Active = false
			FX.VeilInstance = veil
			resetVeilGeometry()
		end
		veil.BackgroundColor3 = FX.GetBG and FX.GetBG() or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		return veil
	end

	local Styles = {}
	-- 1 Â· SLIDE+ : quint slide with a soft flash
	Styles[1] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		if entering then
			R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
			tween(R.veil, 0.5, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			tween(page, 0.55, Quint, EOut, {Position = goal})
			task.wait(0.55)
		else
			tween(R.veil, 0.4, Quad, EIn, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.6})
			tween(page, 0.42, Quint, EIn, {Position = goal})
			task.wait(0.42)
			tween(R.veil, 0.15, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end
	-- 2 Â· ZOOM : push-in / pull-out
	Styles[2] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal
			R.scale.Scale = 1.28
			R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			tween(R.scale, 0.55, Quint, EOut, {Scale = 1})
			tween(R.veil, 0.5, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			task.wait(0.55)
		else
			tween(R.scale, 0.4, Quint, EIn, {Scale = 0.72})
			tween(R.veil, 0.38, Quad, EIn, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
			task.wait(0.4)
			if not R.alive() then return end
			page.Position = goal
			R.scale.Scale = 1
			tween(R.veil, 0.2, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end
	-- 3 Â· DROP : falls in with a bounce, drops out
	Styles[3] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal + UDim2.fromOffset(0, -260)
			R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
			tween(R.veil, 0.5, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			tween(page, 0.7, Bounce, EOut, {Position = goal})
			task.wait(0.7)
		else
			tween(page, 0.4, Back, EIn, {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 260)})
			tween(R.veil, 0.38, Quad, EIn, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5})
			task.wait(0.4)
			if not R.alive() then return end
			page.Position = goal
			tween(R.veil, 0.15, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end
	-- 4 Â· SPIN : tilt + scale with overshoot
	Styles[4] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal
			page.Rotation = -14
			R.scale.Scale = 0.8
			R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
			tween(page, 0.6, Back, EOut, {Rotation = 0})
			tween(R.scale, 0.6, Back, EOut, {Scale = 1})
			tween(R.veil, 0.45, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			task.wait(0.6)
		else
			tween(page, 0.4, Quad, EIn, {Rotation = 16})
			tween(R.scale, 0.4, Quad, EIn, {Scale = 0.7})
			tween(R.veil, 0.38, Quad, EIn, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
			task.wait(0.4)
			if not R.alive() then return end
			page.Position = goal
			page.Rotation = 0
			R.scale.Scale = 1
			tween(R.veil, 0.2, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end
	-- 5 Â· GLITCH : jitter + flicker, then settle
	Styles[5] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal
			for i = 1, 7 do
				if not R.alive() then return end
				page.Position = goal + UDim2.fromOffset(math.random(-22, 22), math.random(-3, 3))
				page.Rotation = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2.4
				R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = (i % 2 == 0) and 0.2 or 0.85
				task.wait(0.04)
			end
			page.Rotation = 0
			tween(page, 0.18, Quint, EOut, {Position = goal})
			tween(R.veil, 0.2, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			task.wait(0.2)
		else
			for i = 1, 5 do
				if not R.alive() then return end
				page.Position = CENTER + UDim2.fromOffset(math.random(-26, 26), math.random(-3, 3))
				page.Rotation = (math.random() - 0.5) * 3
				R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85 - i * 0.14
				task.wait(0.045)
			end
			page.Position = goal
			page.Rotation = 0
			tween(R.veil, 0.16, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end
	-- 6 Â· CURTAIN : wipe reveal / wipe cover
	Styles[6] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		local v = R.veil
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal
			v.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
			v.Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.5)
			v.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			v.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			tween(v, 0.55, Quint, EInOut, {Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)})
			task.wait(0.55)
			v.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		else
			v.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			v.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
			v.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
			v.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			tween(v, 0.4, Quint, EInOut, {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)})
			task.wait(0.4)
			if not R.alive() then return end
			page.Position = goal
			tween(v, 0.18, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end

	-- â”€â”€ helpers for the multi-piece transitions â”€â”€
	local function bgColor()
		return FX.GetBG and FX.GetBG() or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
	local function accentColor()
		return FX.GetColor and FX.GetColor() or WHITE
	end
	local function dropCells(cells)
		for _, c in ipairs(cells) do
			tween(c, 0.15, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			Debris:AddItem(c, 0.3)
		end
	end
	local function makeGrid(cols, rows, startTransparency)
		local cells = {}
		local bg = bgColor()
		for y = 0, rows - 1 do
			for x = 0, cols - 1 do
				local c = Util.Instance("Frame", FX.Content)
				c.Position = UDim2.fromScale(x / cols, y / rows)
				c.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 / cols + 0.003, 1 / rows + 0.003)
				c.BackgroundColor3 = bg
				c.BackgroundTransparency = startTransparency
				c.BorderSizePixel = 0
				c.ZIndex = 100
				c.Interactable = false
				cells[#cells + 1] = c
				Debris:AddItem(c, 2)
			end
		end
		return cells
	end

	-- 7 Â· BLINDS : venetian slats snap shut / open (NEW)
	Styles[7] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		local N = 8
		local full = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1 / N + 0.004, 0)
		local none = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		local slats = {}
		local bg = bgColor()
		for i = 1, N do
			local s = Util.Instance("Frame", FX.Content)
			s.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, (i - 1) / N)
			s.Size = entering and full or none
			s.BackgroundColor3 = bg
			s.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			s.BorderSizePixel = 0
			s.ZIndex = 100
			s.Interactable = false
			slats[i] = s
			Debris:AddItem(s, 2)
		end
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal
			for i, s in ipairs(slats) do
				task.delay((i - 1) * 0.04, function()
					tween(s, 0.3, Quad, EOut, {Size = none})
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.3 + N * 0.04)
		else
			for i, s in ipairs(slats) do
				task.delay((i - 1) * 0.03, function()
					tween(s, 0.22, Quad, EIn, {Size = full})
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.22 + N * 0.03)
			if not R.alive() then
				dropCells(slats)
				return
			end
			page.Position = goal
			dropCells(slats)
			task.wait(0.15)
		end
	end

	-- 8 Â· PIXEL DISSOLVE : a grid of tiles flickers in / out in random order (NEW)
	Styles[8] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		local cells = makeGrid(8, 5, entering and 0 or 1)
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal
			for _, c in ipairs(cells) do
				task.delay(math.random() * 0.32, function()
					tween(c, 0.2, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.55)
		else
			for _, c in ipairs(cells) do
				task.delay(math.random() * 0.22, function()
					tween(c, 0.16, Quad, EIn, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.42)
			if not R.alive() then
				dropCells(cells)
				return
			end
			page.Position = goal
			for _, c in ipairs(cells) do
				task.delay(math.random() * 0.12, function()
					tween(c, 0.16, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.3)
		end
	end

	-- 9 Â· ELASTIC : springy slide-in, snappy back-ease out (NEW)
	Styles[9] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal + UDim2.fromOffset(380, 0)
			R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
			tween(R.veil, 0.45, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			tween(page, 0.85, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic, EOut, {Position = goal})
			task.wait(0.85)
		else
			tween(R.veil, 0.35, Quad, EIn, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5})
			tween(page, 0.38, Back, EIn, {Position = goal})
			task.wait(0.38)
			tween(R.veil, 0.15, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end

	-- 10 Â· SCAN SWEEP : a bright scan line wipes the page in / out (NEW)
	Styles[10] = function(page, entering, goal, R)
		local v = R.veil
		local line = Util.Instance("Frame", FX.Content)
		line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		line.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
		line.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
		line.BorderSizePixel = 0
		line.BackgroundColor3 = accentColor()
		line.ZIndex = 101
		line.Interactable = false
		Debris:AddItem(line, 1.2)
		if entering then
			page.Position = goal
			v.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
			v.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1)
			v.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			v.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			tween(v, 0.6, Quint, EInOut, {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)})
			tween(line, 0.6, Quint, EInOut, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)})
			task.wait(0.6)
			v.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			tween(line, 0.15, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		else
			v.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
			v.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0)
			v.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
			v.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			tween(v, 0.4, Quint, EInOut, {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)})
			tween(line, 0.4, Quint, EInOut, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)})
			task.wait(0.4)
			if not R.alive() then
				line:Destroy()
				return
			end
			page.Position = goal
			tween(line, 0.15, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
			tween(v, 0.18, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		end
	end

	-- Tween-like handle so the script's  tween:Play() / tween.Completed:Connect()  keep working
	local function pageTween(page, props)
		local goal = props.Position
		local entering = goal.X.Offset == 0 and goal.Y.Offset == 0
		local styleId = PageStyle[page] or assignStyle(page)
		local handle = {PlaybackState = Enum.PlaybackState.Begin}
		local done = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		handle.Completed = done.Event
		local started, cancelled = false, false

		local function run()
			local token = (Active[page] or 0) + 1
			Active[page] = token
			local rig = getRig(page)
			local R = {scale = rig.scale, veil = getVeil()}
			R.alive = function()
				return Active[page] == token and not cancelled
			end
			veilToken = veilToken + 1
			local myVeil = veilToken
			resetVeilGeometry()
			R.veil.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			FX.BlurPulse(entering and 7 or 5, 0.14, 0.5)
			FX.Play(entering and "open" or "back")
			if entering then pcall(FX.Cascade, page) end
			local ok, err = pcall(Styles[styleId] or Styles[1], page, entering, goal, R)
			if not ok then warn("[UhhhhhhFX] transition error: " .. tostring(err)) end
			if not R.alive() then return false end
			page.Position = goal
			page.Rotation = 0
			R.scale.Scale = 1
			task.delay(0.3, function()
				if veilToken == myVeil then
					resetVeilGeometry()
				end
			end)
			return true
		end
		function handle:Play()
			if started then return end
			started = true
			self.PlaybackState = Enum.PlaybackState.Playing
			task.defer(function()
				local completed = run()
				if completed and not cancelled then
					self.PlaybackState = Enum.PlaybackState.Completed
					done:Fire(Enum.PlaybackState.Completed)
				end
			end)
		end
		function handle:Cancel()
			cancelled = true
			self.PlaybackState = Enum.PlaybackState.Cancelled
		end
		function handle:Pause() end
		function handle:Destroy()
			cancelled = true
		end
		return handle
	end

	-- replace the script's TweenService with a proxy that upgrades page transitions
	local Proxy = setmetatable({}, {
		__index = function(_, k)
			local v = RealTweenService[k]
			if type(v) == "function" then
				return function(_, ...)
					return v(RealTweenService, ...)
				end
			end
			return v
		end,
	})
	function Proxy.Create(_, obj, info, props)
		if props and typeof(obj) == "Instance" then
			if FX.Settings.Transitions and FX.Content and props.Position and next(props, (next(props))) == nil
				and obj.Parent == FX.Content and obj ~= FX.VeilInstance then
				return pageTween(obj, props)
			end
			if obj == FX.Window then
				FX.BlurPulse(6, 0.12, 0.5)
				local small = props.Size and props.Size.X.Offset < 200
				FX.Play(small and "close" or "open")
			end
		end
		return RealTweenService:Create(obj, info, props)
	end
	TweenService = Proxy

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� REMASTERED INTRO â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	local NAME = "STEVETHEREALONE"
	local GLYPHS = "01#%&$@?/\\<>"
	local function glyphs(n)
		local s = ""
		for _ = 1, n do
			local k = math.random(1, #GLYPHS)
			s = s .. GLYPHS:sub(k, k)
		end
		return s
	end

	function FX.Intro(MainFrame, Music, marquee)
		-- v2: shards assemble into the triangle, god-rays, HUD corners, beat rings, glitch title.
		-- Cue times are the original music sync points. Click / tap / any key skips it.
		local DUR, CLIMAX, CUE = 5.256, 4.256, 2.152
		local began = os.clock()
		local CFG = FX.Cfg
		local NAME = (tostring(CFG.IntroTitle):gsub("[^\32-\126]", "")):sub(1, 20)
		if NAME == "" then NAME = "STEVETHEREALONE" end
		local INTENSITY = CFG.IntroIntensity
		local function accent()
			if CFG.IntroColorMode == 2 then
				return Util.ParseHex(CFG.IntroColor) or WHITE
			elseif CFG.IntroColorMode == 3 then
				return WHITE
			end
			return Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() / 10) % 1, 0.8, 1)
		end

		local stage = Util.Instance("Frame", MainFrame)
		stage.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		stage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		stage.BorderSizePixel = 0
		stage.Interactable = false
		local fade = tween(MainFrame, 5, Linear, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.4})

		local skipped = false
		local skipConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if os.clock() - began < 0.5 then return end
			local ty = input.UserInputType
			if ty == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ty == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or ty == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				skipped = true
			end
		end)

		-- marquee (kept from the original intro)
		local scrolltext = Util.MakeText(marquee)
		scrolltext.Parent = stage
		scrolltext.ZIndex = 2
		scrolltext.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		scrolltext.Position = UDim2.new(1.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		Util.SetTextColor(scrolltext, WHITE, 0.78)
		tween(scrolltext, 5.256, Linear, EOut, {Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)})
		local ratio = scrolltext.Size.X.Offset / scrolltext.Size.Y.Offset

		-- god-rays behind the triangle
		local rays = {}
		for i = 1, 14 do
			local f = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
			f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			f.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 2)
			f.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
			f.BorderSizePixel = 0
			f.ZIndex = 1
			f.Visible = false
			f.Interactable = false
			local g = Util.Instance("UIGradient", f)
			g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.35), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
			rays[i] = {f = f, i = i, a = (i / 14) * math.pi * 2, w = ((i % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1) * (0.12 + (i % 5) * 0.04), th = 2 + (i % 3)}
		end

		-- warp field
		local stars = {}
		for i = 1, (CFG.IntroStars and 64 or 0) do
			local f = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
			f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			f.BorderSizePixel = 0
			f.ZIndex = 1
			f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2)
			f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			f.Interactable = false
			stars[i] = {f = f, i = i, a = math.random() * math.pi * 2, r = math.random(), v = 0.15 + math.random() * 0.35}
		end

		-- scanline sweep
		local scan = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
		scan.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		scan.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 70)
		scan.BorderSizePixel = 0
		scan.ZIndex = 4
		scan.Visible = false
		scan.Interactable = false
		local sg = Util.Instance("UIGradient", scan)
		sg.Rotation = 90
		sg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.55)})
		local scanLine = Util.Instance("Frame", scan)
		scanLine.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
		scanLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
		scanLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
		scanLine.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		scanLine.BorderSizePixel = 0

		-- 3D triangle: starts exploded and ASSEMBLES (driven through tri.env below)
		local tri = FX.NewTriangle3D(stage, {Interactive = false, ZIndex = 3})
		tri.yawVel = 16
		tri.scaleBase = 0.6
		tri.env, tri.extra = 1, 1

		-- letterbox
		local barTop = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
		barTop.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		barTop.BorderSizePixel = 0
		barTop.ZIndex = 6
		barTop.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		barTop.Interactable = false
		local barBot = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
		barBot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
		barBot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
		barBot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		barBot.BorderSizePixel = 0
		barBot.ZIndex = 6
		barBot.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		barBot.Interactable = false

		-- HUD corner brackets
		local hud = {}
		local function bracket(ax, ay)
			local host = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
			host.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(ax, ay)
			host.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34)
			host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			host.BorderSizePixel = 0
			host.ZIndex = 7
			host.Interactable = false
			local h = Util.Instance("Frame", host)
			h.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(ax, ay)
			h.Position = UDim2.fromScale(ax, ay)
			h.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
			h.BorderSizePixel = 0
			h.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
			h.ZIndex = 7
			local v = Util.Instance("Frame", host)
			v.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(ax, ay)
			v.Position = UDim2.fromScale(ax, ay)
			v.Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0)
			v.BorderSizePixel = 0
			v.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
			v.ZIndex = 7
			hud[#hud + 1] = {host = host, h = h, v = v, ax = ax, ay = ay}
		end
		bracket(0, 0)
		bracket(1, 0)
		bracket(0, 1)
		bracket(1, 1)
		local function hudText(ax, ay, pos, size)
			local l = Util.Instance("TextLabel", stage)
			l.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(ax, ay)
			l.Position = pos
			l.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 16)
			l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			l.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			l.TextSize = size or 13
			l.TextXAlignment = ax == 0 and Enum.TextXAlignment.Left or (ax == 1 and Enum.TextXAlignment.Right or Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
			l.TextColor3 = WHITE
			l.TextTransparency = 1
			l.ZIndex = 8
			l.Text = ""
			return l
		end
		local counter = hudText(0, 1, UDim2.new(0, 64, 1, -32))
		local verTag = hudText(1, 0, UDim2.new(1, -64, 0, 30))
		verTag.Text = "NOOBY'S UH MODDED REANIMATION // " .. UhhhhhhVersion
		local skipTag = hudText(0.5, 1, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -12), 12)
		skipTag.Text = "click to skip"
		if not CFG.IntroHud then
			for _, b in ipairs(hud) do
				b.host.Visible = false
			end
			counter.Visible = false
			verTag.Visible = false
		end

		-- title (chromatic split) + underline + subtitle
		local box = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
		box.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		box.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.79)
		box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 56)
		box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		box.BorderSizePixel = 0
		box.ZIndex = 5
		box.Visible = false
		box.Interactable = false
		local boxScale = Util.Instance("UIScale", box)
		local function titleLabel(z, color, tr)
			local l = Util.Instance("TextLabel", box)
			l.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			l.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			l.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			l.Font = Enum.Font.Arcade
			l.TextSize = 40
			l.TextColor3 = color
			l.TextTransparency = tr
			l.ZIndex = z
			l.Text = ""
			return l
		end
		local lblR = titleLabel(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 80), 0.35)
		local lblB = titleLabel(2, Color3.fromRGB(40, 200, 255), 0.35)
		local lblM = titleLabel(3, WHITE, 0)
		local ul = Util.Instance("Frame", stage)
		ul.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		ul.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.79, 32)
		ul.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)
		ul.BorderSizePixel = 0
		ul.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		ul.ZIndex = 5
		ul.Interactable = false
		local sub = Util.Instance("TextLabel", stage)
		sub.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		sub.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.885)
		sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
		sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		sub.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		sub.TextSize = 14
		sub.ZIndex = 8
		sub.TextTransparency = 1
		sub.Text = (CFG.IntroSub ~= "" and (tostring(CFG.IntroSub):gsub("[^\32-\126]", ""):sub(1, 80)))
			or ("NOOBY'S UH MODDED REANIMATION  //  " .. UhhhhhhVersion .. "  //  ORIGINAL BY STEVETHEREALONE")

		local shake, bloomed, climaxed = 0, false, false
		local beatsDone = {}
		local BEATS = {0.05, 1.1, 3.2}

		local function run()
			while true do
				local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				local el = os.clock() - began
				local t = Music.TimePosition
				if t < 0.01 and el > 0.8 then t = el - 0.8 end -- music failed to start: fall back to wall clock
				if skipped or t >= DUR or el > 9 then break end
				local col = accent()
				local sw = Util.GetScreenSize()
				local maxR = math.sqrt(sw.X * sw.X + sw.Y * sw.Y) * 0.5
				local height = sw.Y / 3
				scrolltext.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(height * ratio * 0.5, height)
				local out = math.clamp((t - CLIMAX) / 0.8, 0, 1)

				-- warp field
				local warp = (math.clamp(t / DUR, 0, 1) ^ 2) * 4 + (t >= CLIMAX and (t - CLIMAX) * 18 or 0)
				for _, s in ipairs(stars) do
					s.r = s.r + s.v * dt * (0.5 + warp)
					if s.r > 1.05 then
						s.r = math.random() * 0.05
						s.a = math.random() * math.pi * 2
					end
					local d = s.r * maxR
					s.f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(s.a) * d, 0.5, math.sin(s.a) * d)
					s.f.Rotation = math.deg(s.a)
					s.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2 + s.r * warp * 26, 2)
					s.f.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - math.min(1, s.r * 3) * 0.75
					s.f.BackgroundColor3 = (s.i % 5 == 0) and col or WHITE
				end

				-- god-rays
				local rayIn = easeOutCubic(math.clamp((t - 0.25) / 1.6, 0, 1)) * (1 - out) * (CFG.IntroRays and 1 or 0)
				for _, r in ipairs(rays) do
					r.f.Visible = rayIn > 0.01
					if rayIn > 0.01 then
						r.f.Rotation = math.deg(r.a + t * r.w)
						r.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(maxR * rayIn * (0.5 + 0.5 * rayIn), r.th)
						r.f.BackgroundColor3 = (r.i % 3 == 0) and col or WHITE
					end
				end

				-- scanline
				local sy = nil
				if not CFG.IntroScan then
					sy = nil
				elseif t < 1.3 then
					sy = easeInOut(t / 1.3)
				elseif t >= CLIMAX - 0.1 and t < CLIMAX + 0.9 then
					sy = easeInOut((t - (CLIMAX - 0.1)) / 1.0)
				end
				scan.Visible = sy ~= nil
				if sy then
					scan.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, sy * 1.15 - 0.1, -70)
					scan.BackgroundColor3 = col
				end

				-- letterbox
				local bar = 0.11 * easeOutCubic(math.min(1, t)) * (1 - math.clamp((t - CLIMAX) / 0.6, 0, 1)) * (CFG.IntroScan and 1 or 0)
				barTop.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, bar, 0)
				barBot.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, bar, 0)

				-- HUD
				local hin = easeOutCubic(math.clamp((t - 0.2) / 0.9, 0, 1))
				local inset = 24 + (1 - hin) * 80
				for _, b in ipairs(hud) do
					b.host.Position = UDim2.new(b.ax, b.ax == 0 and inset or -inset, b.ay, b.ay == 0 and inset or -inset)
					local tr = math.max(1 - hin, out)
					if math.random() < 0.04 then tr = math.min(1, tr + 0.5) end
					b.h.BackgroundTransparency, b.v.BackgroundTransparency = tr, tr
					b.h.BackgroundColor3, b.v.BackgroundColor3 = col, col
				end
				counter.Text = string.format("SYS.INIT  %03d%%", math.floor(math.clamp(t / DUR, 0, 1) * 100))
				counter.TextColor3, verTag.TextColor3 = col, col
				counter.TextTransparency = math.max(1 - hin * 0.85, out)
				verTag.TextTransparency = math.max(1 - hin * 0.6, out)
				skipTag.TextTransparency = math.max(1 - easeOutCubic(math.clamp((t - 0.8) / 0.5, 0, 1)) * 0.55, out)

				-- triangle: assembles from shards, then fly-through at the climax
				local asm = easeOutCubic(math.clamp((t - 0.1) / 2.0, 0, 1))
				tri.env, tri.extra = 1 - asm, 1 - asm
				tri.yawVel = 2 + (1 - asm) * 14
				tri.scaleBase = 0.6 + 0.4 * asm
				tri.chargeVis = math.clamp((t - 3.0) / 1.2, 0, 1)
				if t >= CLIMAX then
					tri.scaleBase = 1 + (t - CLIMAX) * 13
				end

				-- key moments
				for i, bt in ipairs(BEATS) do
					if not beatsDone[i] and t >= bt then
						beatsDone[i] = true
						FX.Ring(stage, col, 340 + i * 70, 0.8, 3)
						shake = math.max(shake, 5)
					end
				end
				if not bloomed and t >= CUE then
					bloomed = true
					tri:Bloom()
					FX.Ring(stage, col, 520, 0.9, 5)
					FX.Ring(stage, WHITE, 340, 0.7, 2)
					FX.BlurPulse(10, 0.08, 0.7)
					shake = 14
				end
				if not climaxed and t >= CLIMAX then
					climaxed = true
					FX.Ring(stage, WHITE, 760, 0.8, 6)
					FX.Ring(stage, col, 520, 0.7, 4)
					FX.BlurPulse(12, 0.08, 0.8)
					shake = 22
				end
				shake = math.max(0, shake - dt * 40)
				stage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((math.random() - 0.5) * shake * INTENSITY, (math.random() - 0.5) * shake * INTENSITY)

				-- title
				if t >= CUE then
					box.Visible = true
					local n = math.floor((t - CUE) / 0.07)
					local txt
					if n >= #NAME then
						txt = NAME
						if math.random() < 0.05 then
							local k = math.random(1, #NAME)
							txt = NAME:sub(1, k - 1) .. glyphs(1) .. NAME:sub(k + 1)
						end
					else
						txt = NAME:sub(1, n) .. glyphs(math.min(3, #NAME - n))
					end
					lblM.Text, lblR.Text, lblB.Text = txt, txt, txt
					local jx = (math.random() < 0.06) and math.random(-7, 7) or 0
					lblR.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -3 + jx, 0.5, 0)
					lblB.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 3 - jx, 0.5, 0)
					boxScale.Scale = 1 + (1 - easeOutCubic(math.min(1, (t - CUE) / 0.35))) * 0.5 + out * 1.2
					lblM.TextTransparency, lblR.TextTransparency, lblB.TextTransparency = out, 0.35 + out * 0.65, 0.35 + out * 0.65
				end
				local ulp = easeOutCubic(math.clamp((t - (CUE + 1.1)) / 0.7, 0, 1))
				ul.Size = UDim2.new(0.46 * ulp, 0, 0, 2)
				ul.BackgroundColor3 = col
				ul.BackgroundTransparency = out
				sub.TextColor3 = col
				sub.TextTransparency = math.max(1 - easeOutCubic(math.clamp((t - 3.0) / 0.6, 0, 1)), out)
			end
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(run)
		if not ok then
			warn("[UhhhhhhFX] intro error (continuing): " .. tostring(err))
		end

		-- finale: white flash + rings + blur burst (always runs, even after a skip or an error)
		skipConn:Disconnect()
		pcall(function()
			tri:Destroy()
		end)
		stage:Destroy()
		fade:Cancel()
		MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		local flash = Util.Instance("Frame", MainFrame)
		flash.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		flash.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(INTENSITY, 0, 1)
		flash.BorderSizePixel = 0
		flash.Interactable = false
		flash.ZIndex = 256
		tween(flash, 1, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		Debris:AddItem(flash, 1)
		FX.Ring(MainFrame, accent(), 1000, 0.9, 6)
		FX.Ring(MainFrame, WHITE, 700, 0.7, 3)
		FX.BlurPulse(16, 0.04, 1.0)
	end


	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� 3D LOADING SCREEN â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	function FX.BuildLoader(host, env)
		local L = {Visible = false, p = 0, target = 0}
		local showToken = 0
		local capT = 0

		local root = Util.Instance("Frame", host)
		root.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		root.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		root.BorderSizePixel = 0
		root.Visible = false
		root.ZIndex = 40
		root.ClipsDescendants = true

		local function label(size, ax, ay, pos, sz)
			local l = Util.Instance("TextLabel", root)
			l.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(ax, ay)
			l.Position = pos
			l.Size = sz
			l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			l.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			l.TextSize = size
			l.TextColor3 = env.UITextColor and env.UITextColor.Value or WHITE
			l.ZIndex = 5
			l.Text = ""
			return l
		end
		local hint = label(12, 0.5, 0, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8), UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14))
		hint.Text = "drag  Â·  tap  Â·  hold 5s"
		hint.TextTransparency = 0.55
		local cap = label(14, 0.5, 1, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -50), UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16))
		local status = label(13, 0.5, 1, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -20), UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16))
		status.Text = "Loading modules..."
		if env.UITextColor then
			env.UITextColor.Changed:Connect(function(v)
				hint.TextColor3, cap.TextColor3, status.TextColor3 = v, v, v
			end)
		end

		local chargeTrack = Util.Instance("Frame", root)
		chargeTrack.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
		chargeTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -44)
		chargeTrack.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 2)
		chargeTrack.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		chargeTrack.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		chargeTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
		chargeTrack.ZIndex = 5
		local chargeFill = Util.Instance("Frame", chargeTrack)
		chargeFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
		chargeFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
		chargeFill.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		chargeFill.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		chargeFill.ZIndex = 6

		local track = Util.Instance("Frame", root)
		track.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
		track.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -10)
		track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 3)
		track.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		track.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
		track.BorderSizePixel = 0
		track.ZIndex = 5
		local fill = Util.Instance("Frame", track)
		fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
		fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
		fill.ZIndex = 6

		local tri = FX.NewTriangle3D(root, {
			Interactive = true, ZIndex = 2, Ring = true, Track = true, RingSize = 176,
			HitSize = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 150),
			IsVisible = function() return root.Visible end,
			OnAction = function(name, n)
				cap.Text = (n > 0 and (n .. "/5  ") or "") .. name
				capT = 1.4
				cap.TextTransparency = 0
			end,
		})
		tri:SetActive(false)
		L.Triangle = tri

		RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			if not L.Visible and root.BackgroundTransparency >= 0.999 then return end
			local t = env.Time and env.Time() or os.clock()
			if env.GetUIBGColor then root.BackgroundColor3 = env.GetUIBGColor(t) end
			local c = env.GetUIColor and env.GetUIColor(t) or WHITE
			fill.BackgroundColor3 = c
			chargeFill.BackgroundColor3 = c
			L.p = L.p + (L.target - L.p) * (1 - math.exp(-8 * dt))
			fill.Size = UDim2.new(L.p, 0, 1, 0)
			local ch = tri.charge
			chargeFill.Size = UDim2.new(ch, 0, 1, 0)
			chargeFill.BackgroundTransparency = ch > 0.02 and 0 or 1
			chargeTrack.BackgroundTransparency = ch > 0.02 and 0.8 or 1
			if ch > 0.03 and tri.phase == "idle" then
				cap.Text = string.format("HOLD  %d%%", math.floor(ch * 100))
				capT = 0.4
			end
			capT = math.max(0, capT - dt)
			cap.TextTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(capT / 0.4, 0, 1)
		end)

		function L.Show()
			if L.Visible then return end
			L.Visible = true
			showToken = showToken + 1
			L.p, L.target = 0, 0
			root.Visible = true
			tri:SetActive(true)
			tri.scaleBase = 1
			tri:PopIn()
			tween(root, 0.35, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.08})
			FX.BlurPulse(6, 0.12, 0.4)
			FX.Play("open", 1, 0.8)
		end
		function L.Hide()
			if not L.Visible then return end
			L.Visible = false
			showToken = showToken + 1
			local my = showToken
			L.target = 1
			tri:Finale()
			FX.BlurPulse(5, 0.1, 0.5)
			task.delay(0.45, function()
				if my ~= showToken then return end
				tween(root, 0.45, Quad, EOut, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
				task.delay(0.5, function()
					if my ~= showToken then return end
					root.Visible = false
					tri:SetActive(false)
				end)
			end)
		end
		function L.SetStatus(text, prog)
			if typeof(text) == "string" then status.Text = text end
			if type(prog) == "number" then
				L.target = math.clamp(prog, 0, 1)
			end
		end
		return L
	end
	function FX.LoaderStatus(text, prog)
		if FX.Loader and FX.Loader.Visible then
			FX.Loader.SetStatus(text, prog)
		end
	end

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� HOME-SCREEN HERO TRIANGLE â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	-- The cool-scene triangle is now the real interactive 3D one: tap = 5 actions (cycles),
	-- drag = rotate, hold 5s = overload -> explode -> reform. Leans toward the cursor and
	-- pulses with the music. Click anywhere ELSE on the scene to enter (as before).
	function FX.MountHero(frame, textColor, music)
		local cap = Util.Instance("TextLabel", frame)
		cap.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		cap.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 7)
		cap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16)
		cap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		cap.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		cap.TextSize = 14
		cap.TextColor3 = textColor and textColor.Value or WHITE
		cap.TextTransparency = 1
		cap.Text = ""
		cap.ZIndex = 6
		cap.Interactable = false
		if textColor then
			textColor.Changed:Connect(function(v)
				cap.TextColor3 = v
			end)
		end

		-- HUD: corner brackets + live FPS readout
		local brackets = {}
		for _, c in ipairs({{0, 0}, {1, 0}, {0, 1}, {1, 1}}) do
			local ax, ay = c[1], c[2]
			for k = 1, 2 do
				local f = Util.Instance("Frame", frame)
				f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(ax, ay)
				f.Position = UDim2.new(ax, ax == 0 and 6 or -6, ay, ay == 0 and 6 or -6)
				f.Size = (k == 1) and UDim2.fromOffset(14, 1) or UDim2.fromOffset(1, 14)
				f.BorderSizePixel = 0
				f.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
				f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
				f.ZIndex = 6
				f.Interactable = false
				brackets[#brackets + 1] = f
			end
		end
		local stat = Util.Instance("TextLabel", frame)
		stat.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
		stat.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 8)
		stat.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 12)
		stat.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		stat.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		stat.TextSize = 11
		stat.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		stat.TextColor3 = textColor and textColor.Value or WHITE
		stat.TextTransparency = 0.5
		stat.Text = ""
		stat.ZIndex = 6
		stat.Interactable = false
		if textColor then
			textColor.Changed:Connect(function(v)
				stat.TextColor3 = v
			end)
		end
		local fps, fpsT = 60, 0

		local capT, used = 0, false
		local hero
		hero = FX.NewTriangle3D(frame, {
			Interactive = true, ZIndex = 2, Ring = true, Track = true, Music = music,
			RingSize = 176,
			HitSize = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 150),
			IsVisible = function()
				return Util.IsGuiVisible(frame)
			end,
			OnAction = function(name, n)
				used = true
				cap.Text = (n > 0 and (n .. "/5  ") or "") .. name
				capT = 1.6
			end,
		})

		local stuck = 0
		RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			if not frame.Parent then return end
			-- watchdog: a page transition whose Completed never fired used to leave this scene
			-- permanently Interactable=false (dead triangle + dead click-to-start)
			if frame.Visible and not frame.Interactable and frame.Position == UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) then
				stuck = stuck + dt
				if stuck > 0.9 then
					frame.Interactable = true
					stuck = 0
				end
			else
				stuck = 0
			end
			if not Util.IsGuiVisible(frame) then return end
			fps = fps + (1 / math.max(dt, 1e-3) - fps) * 0.08
			fpsT = fpsT + dt
			if fpsT > 0.25 then
				fpsT = 0
				stat.Text = string.format("%d FPS", math.floor(fps + 0.5))
			end
			local accent = FX.GetColor and FX.GetColor() or WHITE
			for _, f in ipairs(brackets) do
				f.BackgroundColor3 = accent
			end
			if hero.charge > 0.03 and hero.phase == "idle" then
				cap.Text = string.format("HOLD  %d%%", math.floor(hero.charge * 100))
				capT = 0.4
			end
			capT = math.max(0, capT - dt)
			if capT > 0 then
				cap.TextTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(capT / 0.4, 0, 1)
			elseif not used then
				cap.Text = "tap  Â·  drag  Â·  hold 5s"
				cap.TextTransparency = 0.55 + math.sin(os.clock() * 2) * 0.12
			else
				cap.TextTransparency = 1
			end
		end)
		hero:PopIn()
		FX.Hero = hero
		return hero
	end

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� WINDOW BOOT-UP â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	-- PreBoot collapses the window to a small title bar the moment it is created (no one-frame flash);
	-- Chrome then expands it. Failsafe restores the size if anything goes wrong.
	function FX.PreBoot(win)
		FX.BootFull = win.Size
		win.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 30)
		task.delay(12, function()
			if win.Parent and win.Size.X.Offset == 44 and win.Size.Y.Offset == 30 then
				win.Size = FX.BootFull
			end
		end)
	end

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� CHROME / AMBIENT LAYER â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	function FX.Chrome(env)
		local win, content = env.UIMainWindow, env.WindowContent
		local music = env.UISound and env.UISound.Music
		if not win or not content then return end
		local S = FX.Settings
		local function guard(name, f)
			local ok, e = pcall(f)
			if not ok then warn("[UhhhhhhFX] " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(e)) end
		end

		local topBar
		for _, c in ipairs(win:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("Frame") and c.Size.Y.Offset == 30 and c.Size.X.Scale == 1 then
				topBar = c
				break
			end
		end

		-- ANIM Â· boot-up: title bar stretches open, then the body unfolds with an overshoot
		task.spawn(function()
			guard("boot", function()
				local full = FX.BootFull or win.Size
				if win.Size.X.Offset > 60 then return end
				if not S.Transitions then
					win.Size = full
					return
				end
				tween(win, 0.32, Quint, EOut, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(full.X.Offset, 30)})
				task.wait(0.32)
				FX.Play("open", 0.9)
				tween(win, 0.6, Back, EOut, {Size = full})
			end)
		end)

		-- top bar: live 3D logo + periodic light shimmer
		guard("topbar", function()
			if not topBar then return end
			local label
			for _, c in ipairs(topBar:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("TextLabel") then
					label = c
					break
				end
			end
			if label then
				label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0.5, 0)
				label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 1, 0)
			end
			local logo = FX.NewTriangle3D(topBar, {
				ZIndex = 5, LayerSize = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 30),
				IsVisible = function()
					return Util.IsGuiVisible(topBar)
				end,
			})
			logo.baseSpin = 1.6
			logo.scaleBase = 1.25
			FX.Logo = logo

			local shim = Util.Instance("Frame", topBar)
			shim.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			shim.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
			shim.BorderSizePixel = 0
			shim.ZIndex = 0
			shim.Interactable = false
			local g = Util.Instance("UIGradient", shim)
			g.Rotation = 20
			g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.86),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
			})
			g.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
			task.spawn(function()
				while shim.Parent do
					task.wait(5 + math.random() * 3)
					if S.Ambient and S.Transitions and Util.IsGuiVisible(topBar) then
						g.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
						tween(g, 0.9, Quad, EInOut, {Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0)})
					end
				end
			end)
		end)

		-- underglow strip along the bottom edge of the window
		local glow, glowGrad
		guard("underglow", function()
			glow = Util.Instance("Frame", win)
			glow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
			glow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -1)
			glow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 2)
			glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
			glow.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
			glow.ZIndex = 4
			glow.Interactable = false
			glowGrad = Util.Instance("UIGradient", glow)
			glowGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, 0.15),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, 0.15),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
			})
		end)

		-- depth vignette: content fades into the title bar / window edge instead of being cut off
		local vig = {}
		guard("vignette", function()
			for _, top in ipairs({true, false}) do
				local f = Util.Instance("Frame", content)
				f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, top and 0 or 1)
				f.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, top and 0 or 1)
				f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
				f.BorderSizePixel = 0
				f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
				f.ZIndex = 50
				f.Interactable = false
				local g = Util.Instance("UIGradient", f)
				g.Rotation = top and 90 or -90
				g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.6), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
				vig[#vig + 1] = f
			end
		end)

		-- ANIM Â· beat pulse: window border + underglow breathe with the music
		local stroke = win:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
		local last, pulse = 0, 0
		RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			if not win.Parent then return end
			pcall(function()
				local t = env.Time and env.Time() or os.clock()
				local bg, col = FX.GetBG(), FX.GetColor()
				for _, f in ipairs(vig) do
					f.BackgroundColor3 = bg
					f.Visible = S.Ambient
				end
				if S.Ambient and music then
					local pbl = music.PlaybackLoudness
					if pbl - last > 20 then pulse = 1 end
					last = pbl
				end
				pulse = math.max(0, pulse - dt * 3.2)
				if stroke then
					stroke.Thickness = S.Ambient and (1 + pulse * 1.8) or 1
				end
				if glow then
					glow.Visible = S.Ambient
					glow.BackgroundColor3 = col
					glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 - pulse * 0.3
					glowGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * 0.7) * 0.45, 0)
				end
			end)
		end)

		-- ANIM Â· click shockwave: a ring leaves the cursor on every press inside the window
		guard("shockwave", function()
			UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if not S.Ambient or not win.Visible then return end
				local ty = input.UserInputType
				if ty ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and ty ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
				local m
				if ty == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					m = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
				else
					m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				end
				local ap, as = win.AbsolutePosition, win.AbsoluteSize
				if m.X < ap.X or m.Y < ap.Y or m.X > ap.X + as.X or m.Y > ap.Y + as.Y then return end
				FX.Ring(win, FX.GetColor(), 64, 0.45, 2, UDim2.fromOffset(m.X - ap.X, m.Y - ap.Y))
			end)
		end)
	end

	-- â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•� HOOK EVERYTHING UP â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�â•�
	function FX.Bind(env)
		local SaveData = env.SaveData
		FX.UISound = env.UISound
		FX.Window = env.UIMainWindow
		FX.Content = env.WindowContent
		FX.GetColor = function()
			return env.GetUIColor(env.Time and env.Time() or os.clock())
		end
		FX.GetBG = function()
			return env.GetUIBGColor(env.Time and env.Time() or os.clock())
		end
		if SaveData then
			FX.Settings.Blur = SaveData.FXBlur ~= false
			FX.Settings.Transitions = SaveData.FXTransitions ~= false
			FX.Settings.ExtraSounds = SaveData.FXSounds ~= false
			FX.Settings.Ambient = SaveData.FXAmbient ~= false
		end
		if env.CracktroFrame then
			assignStyle(env.CracktroFrame, 2)
		end
		task.defer(function()
			local ok, e = pcall(FX.Chrome, env)
			if not ok then warn("[UhhhhhhFX] chrome: " .. tostring(e)) end
		end)

		local UI = env.UI
		if UI then
			local origPage, origButton = UI.CreatePage, UI.CreateButton
			UI.CreatePage = function(...)
				local p = origPage(...)
				if p then assignStyle(p) end
				return p
			end
			UI.CreateButton = function(...)
				local b, txt = origButton(...)
				if b then pcall(FX.EnhanceButton, b) end
				return b, txt
			end
		end

		local oldNotify = Util.UINotify
		if oldNotify then
			Util.UINotify = function(text, prog)
				pcall(FX.LoaderStatus, text, prog)
				return oldNotify(text, prog)
			end
		end

		if env.CracktroFrame and env.IsLoaded then
			FX.Loader = FX.BuildLoader(env.CracktroFrame, env)
			task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					local ok, loaded = pcall(env.IsLoaded)
					if ok then
						if not loaded and not FX.Loader.Visible then
							FX.Loader.Show()
						elseif loaded and FX.Loader.Visible then
							FX.Loader.Hide()
						end
					end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	return FX
end)()

Util.LoopedHSV = function(h, s, v)
	h %= 1
	s = math.clamp(s, 0, 1)
	v = math.clamp(v, 0, 1)
	return Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v)
end
Util.Instance = function(cl, p)
	local i = Instance.new(cl)
	i.Name = Util.RandomString()
	i.Parent = p
	return i
end
Util.LinkDestroyI2C = function(a, b)
	a.Destroying:Once(function()
		b:Disconnect()
	end)
end
Util.LinkDestroyI2I = function(a, b)
	Util.LinkDestroyI2C(b, a.Destroying:Once(function()
		task.defer(b.Destroy, b)
	end))
end
Util.ClearAllChildrenGui = function(a)
	for _,v in a:GetChildren() do
		if v:IsA("GuiObject") then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
end
Util.GetTransparency = function(part)
	return 1 - (1 - part.Transparency) * (1 - part.LocalTransparencyModifier)
end
Util.ScaleCFrame = function(cf, scale)
	return cf + cf.Position * (scale - 1)
end
Util.IsGuiVisible = function(guiObject)
	if not guiObject or not guiObject:IsA("GuiObject") then return false end
	while guiObject do
		if not guiObject.Visible then
			return false
		end
		guiObject = guiObject:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("GuiObject")
	end
	return true
end
Util.IsGuiInteractable = function(guiObject)
	if not guiObject or not guiObject:IsA("GuiObject") then return false end
	while guiObject do
		if not guiObject.Visible or not guiObject.Interactable then
			return false
		end
		guiObject = guiObject:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("GuiObject")
	end
	return true
end
Util.WillGuiSinkInput = function(guiObject)
	return Util.IsGuiInteractable(guiObject) and (guiObject.Active or guiObject:IsA("GuiButton"))
end
Util.UDim2ToVector2Scale = function(x)
	return Vector2.new(x.X.Scale, x.Y.Scale)
end
Util.UDim2ToVector2Offset = function(x)
	return Vector2.new(x.X.Offset, x.Y.Offset)
end
Util.Vector2ToUDim2Scale = function(x)
	return UDim2.fromScale(x.X, x.Y)
end
Util.Vector2ToUDim2Offset = function(x)
	return UDim2.fromOffset(x.X, x.Y)
end
Util.ForceTextSize = function(inst)
	if inst:IsA("TextLabel") then
		local original = inst.TextSize
		local conn conn = RunService.PreRender:Connect(function()
			local size = GuiService.PreferredTextSize.Name
			if size == "Medium" then
				inst.TextSize = original
			elseif size == "Large" then
				inst.TextSize = original - 4
			elseif size == "Larger" then
				inst.TextSize = original - 9
			elseif size == "Largest" then
				inst.TextSize = original - 14
			end
		end)
		Util.LinkDestroyI2C(inst, conn)
	end
end
Util.QueryPlayerSelector = function(query, excludespeaker)
	if #query == 0 then return end
	query = query:lower()
	local t = {}
	for _,player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if player ~= Player or not excludespeaker then
			local a = player.DisplayName:lower():find(query)
			local b = player.Name:lower():find(query)
			if a then table.insert(t, {player, 1, a}) end
			if b then table.insert(t, {player, 0, b}) end
		end
	end
	table.sort(t, function(a, b)
		if a[2] == b[2] then
			return a[3] < b[3]
		end
		return a[2] < b[2]
	end)
	if t[1] then
		return t[1][1]
	end
	return nil
end
